`packages/sites/src/render/link.ts`:

```typescript
import { escapeAttr, escapeHtml, safeUrl } from './escape'

/**
 * The ONLY module in packages/sites that emits an anchor.
 *
 * `safeUrl` returns a plain `string | null`, not a branded type, so a renderer that wrote
 * `` href="${safeUrl(x)}" `` itself would compile, read fine in review, and skip `escapeAttr`
 * entirely. Routing every link through one function makes that mistake unavailable rather
 * than merely tested-against — there is exactly one `href=` in the package, and it is here.
 *
 * A rejected URL drops the link but never the words: the label still renders, as escaped
 * text inside a span (design §5 rule 2 — no dead-or-dangerous link, no lost copy).
 */
export const renderLink = (href: unknown, label: unknown, className: string): string => {
  const url = safeUrl(href)
  if (url === null) return `<span class="${escapeAttr(className)}">${escapeHtml(label)}</span>`
  return `<a class="${escapeAttr(className)}" href="${escapeAttr(url)}">${escapeHtml(label)}</a>`
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/cta.ts`:

```typescript
import { safeUrl } from './escape'
import { renderLink } from './link'

const CTA_CLASS = 'cta'
const CTA_INERT_CLASS = 'cta cta--inert'

/**
 * Shared by hero and offer. A label with no safe href renders as inert text: the copy the
 * model wrote is still readable, but no dead-or-dangerous link ships (design §5 rule 2).
 *
 * This function decides only whether there is a CTA at all and which class it carries —
 * the markup itself comes from {@link renderLink}, which is the package's single
 * link-emitting path. `safeUrl` is called here purely to pick the class; `renderLink`
 * re-derives it, and it is a pure function of its argument.
 */
export const renderCta = (label: string | undefined, href: string | undefined): string => {
  const text = label?.trim() ?? ''
  if (text === '') return ''

  const className = safeUrl(href) === null ? CTA_INERT_CLASS : CTA_CLASS
  return renderLink(href, text, className)
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/sections/hero.ts`:

```typescript
import type { HeroContent } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { renderCta } from '../cta'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--hero'

export const renderHero = (content: HeroContent, _ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const subhead = content.subhead?.trim() ?? ''
  const parts = [
    headline === '' ? '' : `<h1>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h1>`,
    subhead === '' ? '' : `<p class="lede">${escapeHtml(subhead)}</p>`,
    renderCta(content.ctaLabel, content.ctaHref),
  ].filter((part) => part !== '')

  if (parts.length === 0) return ''

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}"><div class="wrap">${parts.join('')}</div></section>`
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/sections/features.ts`:

```typescript
import type { FeatureItem, FeaturesContent } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--features'

const renderItem = (item: FeatureItem): string => {
  const body = item.body?.trim() ?? ''
  const bodyHtml = body === '' ? '' : `<p>${escapeHtml(body)}</p>`
  return `<li><h3>${escapeHtml(item.title.trim())}</h3>${bodyHtml}</li>`
}

export const renderFeatures = (content: FeaturesContent, _ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const items = content.items.filter((item) => (item.title ?? '').trim() !== '')

  if (headline === '' && items.length === 0) return ''

  const list = items.length === 0 ? '' : `<ul class="grid">${items.map(renderItem).join('')}</ul>`
  const heading = headline === '' ? '' : `<h2>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h2>`

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}"><div class="wrap">${heading}${list}</div></section>`
}
```

```typescript
import type { OfferContent } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { renderCta } from '../cta'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--offer'

export const renderOffer = (content: OfferContent, _ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const body = content.body?.trim() ?? ''
  const priceNote = content.priceNote?.trim() ?? ''
  const parts = [
    headline === '' ? '' : `<h2>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h2>`,
    body === '' ? '' : `<p>${escapeHtml(body)}</p>`,
    priceNote === '' ? '' : `<p class="price">${escapeHtml(priceNote)}</p>`,
    renderCta(content.ctaLabel, content.ctaHref),
  ].filter((part) => part !== '')

  if (parts.length === 0) return ''

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}"><div class="wrap">${parts.join('')}</div></section>`
}
```

```typescript
import type { TestimonialItem, TestimonialsContent } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--testimonials'

const renderItem = (item: TestimonialItem): string => {
  const author = item.author?.trim() ?? ''
  const role = item.role?.trim() ?? ''
  const roleHtml = role === '' ? '' : `<span class="role">${escapeHtml(role)}</span>`
  const caption =
    author === '' && role === '' ? '' : `<figcaption>${escapeHtml(author)}${roleHtml}</figcaption>`
  return `<li><figure><blockquote><p>${escapeHtml(item.quote.trim())}</p></blockquote>${caption}</figure></li>`
}

export const renderTestimonials = (content: TestimonialsContent, _ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const items = content.items.filter((item) => (item.quote ?? '').trim() !== '')

  if (headline === '' && items.length === 0) return ''

  const list = items.length === 0 ? '' : `<ul class="grid">${items.map(renderItem).join('')}</ul>`
  const heading = headline === '' ? '' : `<h2>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h2>`

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}"><div class="wrap">${heading}${list}</div></section>`
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/sections/faq.ts`:

```typescript
import type { FaqContent, FaqItem } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--faq'

const renderItem = (item: FaqItem): string =>
  `<dt>${escapeHtml(item.q.trim())}</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(item.a.trim())}</dd>`

export const renderFaq = (content: FaqContent, _ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const items = content.items.filter(
    (item) => (item.q ?? '').trim() !== '' && (item.a ?? '').trim() !== '',
  )

  if (headline === '' && items.length === 0) return ''

  const list = items.length === 0 ? '' : `<dl class="faq">${items.map(renderItem).join('')}</dl>`
  const heading = headline === '' ? '' : `<h2>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h2>`

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}"><div class="wrap">${heading}${list}</div></section>`
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/sections/contact.ts`:

```typescript
import type { ContactContent } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { escapeHtml } from '../escape'
import { renderLeadForm } from '../form'

const SECTION_CLASS = 'section section--contact'
const SECTION_ID = 'contact'

export const renderContact = (content: ContactContent, ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const headline = content.headline?.trim() ?? ''
  const body = content.body?.trim() ?? ''
  // renderLeadForm returns '' when ctx.formAction is null — no dead form ships.
  const form = renderLeadForm(content.submitLabel?.trim() ?? '', ctx)
  const parts = [
    headline === '' ? '' : `<h2>${escapeHtml(headline)}</h2>`,
    body === '' ? '' : `<p>${escapeHtml(body)}</p>`,
    form,
  ].filter((part) => part !== '')

  if (parts.length === 0) return ''

  return `<section class="${SECTION_CLASS}" id="${SECTION_ID}"><div class="wrap">${parts.join('')}</div></section>`
}
```

`packages/sites/src/render/sections/index.ts`:

```typescript
import type { NormalizedSection } from '../../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from '../context'
import { renderHero } from './hero'
import { renderFeatures } from './features'
import { renderOffer } from './offer'
import { renderTestimonials } from './testimonials'
import { renderFaq } from './faq'
import { renderContact } from './contact'

/**
 * The one place a SectionKind becomes markup. The switch is exhaustive over the
 * discriminated union, so adding a kind in @sahoda/shared breaks the build here
 * rather than silently rendering nothing.
 */
export const renderSection = (section: NormalizedSection, ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const node = section.section
  switch (node.kind) {
    case 'hero':
      return renderHero(node.content, ctx)
    case 'features':
      return renderFeatures(node.content, ctx)
    case 'offer':
      return renderOffer(node.content, ctx)
    case 'testimonials':
      return renderTestimonials(node.content, ctx)
    case 'faq':
      return renderFaq(node.content, ctx)
    case 'contact':
      return renderContact(node.content, ctx)
  }
}

export { renderHero, renderFeatures, renderOffer, renderTestimonials, renderFaq, renderContact }
```

### Task 13: Document shell (`src/render/document.ts`)

> **Order note.** `RenderContext` now lives in `src/render/context.ts` (Task 10), not here, so this module imports the type from a leaf and the six renderers' values from Task 12 — a one-way dependency with no cycle of any kind, not even a type-only one. Everything `document.ts` needs already exists by the time this task runs.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/sites/src/render/document.ts`
- Test: `packages/sites/src/render/document.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `escapeHtml(raw: unknown): string`, `escapeAttr(raw: unknown): string`, `safeUrl(raw: unknown): string | null` (Task 1) · `themeCss(tokens: ThemeTokens | null): string` (Task 9) · `DraftPage` (Task 4) · `type RenderContext` from `src/render/context.ts` (Task 10) · `renderSection(section: NormalizedSection, ctx: RenderContext): string` (Task 12)
- Produces: `renderDocument(page: DraftPage, ctx: RenderContext): string` · `renderStylesheet(ctx: RenderContext): string`

**Design decisions this task locks:**
- `<head>` inlines `ctx.tokensCss` + the theme override block in a `<style>` tag (custom properties must land before first paint), and `<link>`s `/styles.css` for layout. No duplication between the two.
- Zero inline JavaScript. The contact form is a plain HTML `POST` (Task 11), which removes the entire inline-JS attack class.
- `<title>` falls back to `ctx.siteName` when the page title is blank (design doc §4 invariant #6).
- mesh emits only `seo.description`, so OG/Twitter tags are synthesized from the title, the description, and `canonicalOrigin`.

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { ThemeTokens } from '@sahoda/shared'
import type { DraftPage } from '../normalize/draft'
import type { NormalizedSection } from '../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from './context'
import { renderDocument, renderStylesheet } from './document'

/** A deliberately tiny stand-in for packages/shared/tokens.css: real tokens.css already
 *  defines --pstrong, which would make the "no theme block" assertion meaningless. */
const TOKENS_CSS = ':root{--ink:#131313;--bg:#ffffff}'

const XSS = '<script>alert(1)</script>'
/**
 * Matches a real QUOTED event-handler attribute (` onclick="`). The trailing quote is
 * load-bearing: correctly escaped output still contains the literal text ` onerror=`,
 * because `x" onerror="alert(1)` escapes to `x&quot; onerror=&quot;alert(1)`. Without the
 * quote this pattern fails on output that is provably safe.
 */
const EVENT_HANDLER = /\son[a-z]+\s*=\s*["']/i

const THEME: ThemeTokens = {
  primary: 'oklch(0.62 0.18 250)',
  primaryFg: 'oklch(1 0 0)',
  secondary: 'oklch(0.5 0.05 250)',
  accent: 'oklch(0.7 0.15 60)',
  surface: ['oklch(1 0 0)', 'oklch(0.98 0 0)', 'oklch(0.95 0 0)', 'oklch(0.9 0 0)'],
  text: { hi: 'oklch(0.2 0 0)', mid: 'oklch(0.45 0 0)', low: 'oklch(0.6 0 0)' },
  border: 'oklch(0.9 0 0)',
  success: 'oklch(0.7 0.15 150)',
  warning: 'oklch(0.8 0.15 80)',
  danger: 'oklch(0.6 0.2 25)',
  radius: '12px',
  fontHeading: 'Outfit',
  fontBody: 'Outfit',
}

const buildCtx = (overrides: Partial<RenderContext> = {}): RenderContext => ({
  siteName: 'Sharma Dental',
  tokensCss: TOKENS_CSS,
  theme: null,
  formAction: '/api/leads/sharma-dental',
  canonicalOrigin: null,
  ...overrides,
})

const heroSection = (headline: string, subhead: string): NormalizedSection => ({
  section: { kind: 'hero', content: { headline, subhead, ctaLabel: 'Book now', ctaHref: '/#contact' } },
  sort: 0,
  raw: { headline, subhead },
})

const buildPage = (overrides: Partial<DraftPage> = {}): DraftPage => ({
  path: '/',
  title: 'Home',
  seoDescription: 'Painless dentistry in Indiranagar.',
  sort: 0,
  sections: [heroSection('Smile brighter', 'Same-day appointments')],
  ...overrides,
})

describe('renderDocument — escaping gate (design §5: a prompt-injected script would run on the tenant origin)', () => {
  it('escapes a script payload in the site name so the <title> cannot execute it', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx({ siteName: XSS }))

    expect(html).not.toContain('<script')
    expect(html).toContain('&lt;script&gt;')
  })

  it('escapes a script payload in the page title so the <title> cannot execute it', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ title: XSS }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).not.toContain('<script')
    expect(html).toContain('&lt;script&gt;')
  })

  it('escapes the seo description in attribute context so it cannot break out of content="…"', () => {
    const payload = 'nice x"><script>alert(1)</script>'
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ seoDescription: payload }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).not.toContain('<script')
    expect(html).not.toContain('"><')
  })

  it('contains no <script tag anywhere even when every text field carries a payload', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(
      buildPage({ title: XSS, seoDescription: XSS, sections: [heroSection(XSS, XSS)] }),
      buildCtx({ siteName: XSS, canonicalOrigin: 'https://sharma.sahoda.site' }),
    )

    expect(html).not.toContain('<script')
  })

  it('contains no event-handler attribute anywhere — v0 ships zero inline JavaScript', () => {
    const payload = 'x" onerror="alert(1)'
    const html = renderDocument(
      buildPage({ title: payload, seoDescription: payload, sections: [heroSection(payload, payload)] }),
      buildCtx({ siteName: payload }),
    )

    expect(html).not.toMatch(EVENT_HANDLER)
  })
})

describe('renderDocument — head shell', () => {
  it('inlines tokens.css verbatim so custom properties resolve before first paint', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain(TOKENS_CSS)
  })

  it('emits no theme override block when the workspace has no active theme (the default path — workspace_themes is never seeded)', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx({ theme: null }))

    expect(html).not.toContain('--pstrong')
  })

  it('emits the derived brand override block when an active theme is present', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx({ theme: THEME }))

    expect(html).toContain('--pstrong')
    expect(html).toContain('--t300')
  })

  it('emits a viewport meta so generated sites are responsive always (docs/06 §4.6)', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain('<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">')
  })

  it('emits a lang attribute on <html> so screen readers pick the right voice', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain('<html lang="en">')
  })

  it('falls back to the site name when the page title is blank (invariant #6: title has no .min(1))', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ title: '   ' }), buildCtx({ siteName: 'Sharma Dental' }))

    expect(html).toContain('<title>Sharma Dental</title>')
  })

  it('omits the description meta entirely when mesh emitted no seo block, rather than an empty one', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ seoDescription: null }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).not.toContain('name="description"')
    expect(html).not.toContain('og:description')
  })

  it('synthesises og:title and og:description from the page because mesh only emits seo.description', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ title: 'Home', seoDescription: 'Painless dentistry.' }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain('<meta property="og:title" content="Home">')
    expect(html).toContain('<meta property="og:description" content="Painless dentistry.">')
    expect(html).toContain('<meta property="og:type" content="website">')
  })

  it('omits canonical and og:url when no origin is injected, rather than inventing a plausible URL', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx({ canonicalOrigin: null }))

    expect(html).not.toContain('rel="canonical"')
    expect(html).not.toContain('og:url')
  })

  it('builds canonical and og:url from the injected origin joined to the page path', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(
      buildPage({ path: '/about' }),
      buildCtx({ canonicalOrigin: 'https://sharma.sahoda.site/' }),
    )

    expect(html).toContain('<link rel="canonical" href="https://sharma.sahoda.site/about">')
    expect(html).toContain('<meta property="og:url" content="https://sharma.sahoda.site/about">')
  })

  it('drops canonical when the injected origin is not an http(s) URL, rather than emitting a dangerous href', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage(), buildCtx({ canonicalOrigin: 'javascript:alert(1)' }))

    expect(html).not.toContain('rel="canonical"')
    expect(html).not.toContain('javascript:')
  })

  it('links the root-absolute stylesheet so a nested page does not resolve it under its own folder', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ path: '/about' }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain('<link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css">')
  })

  it('renders each section into <main> in draft order', () => {
    const html = renderDocument(buildPage({ sections: [heroSection('Smile brighter', 'Same-day')] }), buildCtx())

    expect(html).toContain('<main>')
    expect(html).toContain('<h1>Smile brighter</h1>')
  })
})

describe('renderStylesheet — layout sheet, font names allowlisted', () => {
  it('uses the theme font names when they are plain alphanumeric', () => {
    const css = renderStylesheet(buildCtx({ theme: { ...THEME, fontHeading: 'Playfair Display', fontBody: 'Inter' } }))

    expect(css).toContain("--site-font-heading:'Playfair Display'")
    expect(css).toContain("--site-font-body:'Inter'")
  })

  it("falls back to Outfit when a font name carries CSS-breaking characters, so it cannot inject a rule", () => {
    const css = renderStylesheet(buildCtx({ theme: { ...THEME, fontBody: "x';}body{display:none}'" } }))

    expect(css).not.toContain('display:none')
    expect(css).toContain("--site-font-body:'Outfit'")
  })

  it('falls back to Outfit for both families when there is no active theme (docs/07 Brand Kit)', () => {
    const css = renderStylesheet(buildCtx({ theme: null }))

    expect(css).toContain("--site-font-heading:'Outfit'")
    expect(css).toContain("--site-font-body:'Outfit'")
  })

  it('contains a responsive grid rule so section lists reflow on narrow screens', () => {
    const css = renderStylesheet(buildCtx())

    expect(css).toContain('grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit')
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```typescript
import type { DraftPage } from '../normalize/draft'
import { themeCss } from '../theme/css'
// RenderContext lives in its own leaf module (Task 10) so that sections/*.ts never imports
// a type from this file while this file imports their values.
import type { RenderContext } from './context'
import { escapeAttr, escapeHtml, safeUrl } from './escape'
import { renderSection } from './sections'

const LANG = 'en'
const VIEWPORT = 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1'
const STYLESHEET_HREF = '/styles.css'
const TWITTER_CARD = 'summary_large_image'

/** docs/07 Brand Kit:62 — generated sites use Outfit. Static markup, never model-supplied. */
const FONT_LINKS = [
  '<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">',
  '<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>',
  '<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Outfit:wght@400;600;800&display=swap">',
].join('')

const DEFAULT_FONT = 'Outfit'
/** Allowlist: no quote, semicolon or brace can survive, so a theme value cannot inject a rule. */
const FONT_NAME_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 ]{0,39}$/
const FONT_FALLBACKS = "ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif"

const BASE_CSS = [
  '*,*::before,*::after{box-sizing:border-box}',
  'body{margin:0;font-family:var(--site-font-body);color:var(--ink);background:var(--bg);line-height:1.6;-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%}',
  'img,video{max-width:100%;height:auto}',
  '.wrap{width:100%;max-width:72rem;margin:0 auto;padding:0 1.25rem}',
  '.section{padding:clamp(2.5rem,6vw,5rem) 0}',
  'h1,h2,h3{font-family:var(--site-font-heading);line-height:1.15;margin:0 0 .5em}',
  'h1{font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem)}',
  'h2{font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3.5vw,2.25rem)}',
  'h3{font-size:1.125rem;margin-bottom:.35em}',
  'p{margin:0 0 1rem}',
  '.lede{font-size:clamp(1.05rem,2vw,1.25rem)}',
  '.grid{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;gap:1.5rem;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,16rem),1fr))}',
  '.cta{display:inline-block;padding:.75rem 1.5rem;border-radius:12px;background:var(--p);color:var(--pfg);text-decoration:none;font-weight:600}',
  '.cta--inert{background:var(--t100);color:var(--ink)}',
  '.faq{margin:0}',
  '.faq dt{font-weight:600;margin-top:1.25rem}',
  '.faq dd{margin:.25rem 0 0}',
  'figure{margin:0}',
  'blockquote{margin:0 0 .75rem;font-size:1.05rem}',
  '.role::before{content:", "}',
  '.lead-form{display:grid;gap:1rem;max-width:32rem}',
  '.field{display:grid;gap:.35rem}',
  '.field span{font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem}',
  'input,textarea{font:inherit;width:100%;padding:.7rem .8rem;border:1px solid var(--t300);border-radius:8px;background:var(--s1);color:inherit}',
  'button{font:inherit;border:0;cursor:pointer}',
  '.fine{font-size:.85rem;margin:0}',
].join('')

const safeFontName = (raw: unknown): string =>
  typeof raw === 'string' && FONT_NAME_PATTERN.test(raw.trim()) ? raw.trim() : DEFAULT_FONT

const fontRule = (ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const heading = safeFontName(ctx.theme?.fontHeading)
  const body = safeFontName(ctx.theme?.fontBody)
  return `:root{--site-font-heading:'${heading}',${FONT_FALLBACKS};--site-font-body:'${body}',${FONT_FALLBACKS}}`
}

const pickTitle = (rawTitle: string, siteName: string): string => {
  const trimmed = rawTitle.trim()
  return trimmed === '' ? siteName : trimmed
}

const pageUrl = (origin: string | null, path: string): string | null => {
  if (origin === null) return null
  const base = origin.replace(/\/+$/, '')
  return safeUrl(path === '/' ? `${base}/` : `${base}${path}`)
}

/** tokens.css baseline + the seven derived brand vars. Both are trusted, non-model strings. */
const headStyle = (ctx: RenderContext): string => `${ctx.tokensCss}${themeCss(ctx.theme)}`

export const renderStylesheet = (ctx: RenderContext): string => `${fontRule(ctx)}${BASE_CSS}`

export const renderDocument = (page: DraftPage, ctx: RenderContext): string => {
  const title = pickTitle(page.title, ctx.siteName)
  const rawDescription = page.seoDescription?.trim() ?? ''
  const description = rawDescription === '' ? null : rawDescription
  const canonical = pageUrl(ctx.canonicalOrigin, page.path)
  const body = page.sections.map((section) => renderSection(section, ctx)).join('')

  return [
    '<!doctype html>',
    `<html lang="${LANG}">`,
    '<head>',
    '<meta charset="utf-8">',
    `<meta name="viewport" content="${VIEWPORT}">`,
    `<title>${escapeHtml(title)}</title>`,
    description === null ? '' : `<meta name="description" content="${escapeAttr(description)}">`,
    '<meta property="og:type" content="website">',
    `<meta property="og:site_name" content="${escapeAttr(ctx.siteName)}">`,
    `<meta property="og:title" content="${escapeAttr(title)}">`,
    description === null ? '' : `<meta property="og:description" content="${escapeAttr(description)}">`,
    canonical === null ? '' : `<meta property="og:url" content="${escapeAttr(canonical)}">`,
    canonical === null ? '' : `<link rel="canonical" href="${escapeAttr(canonical)}">`,
    `<meta name="twitter:card" content="${TWITTER_CARD}">`,
    FONT_LINKS,
    `<style>${headStyle(ctx)}</style>`,
    `<link rel="stylesheet" href="${STYLESHEET_HREF}">`,
    '</head>',
    '<body>',
    '<main>',
    body,
    '</main>',
    `<footer class="section"><div class="wrap"><p class="fine">© ${escapeHtml(ctx.siteName)}</p></div></footer>`,
    '</body>',
    '</html>',
  ]
    .filter((line) => line !== '')
    .join('')
}
```

### Task 14: Bundle assembly (`src/render/bundle.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/sites/src/render/bundle.ts`
- Test: `packages/sites/src/render/bundle.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pathToFile(path: string): string` (Task 2) · `SiteDraft` (Task 4) · `type RenderContext` from `src/render/context.ts` and `type BundleFile`, `type SiteBundle` from `src/deploy/port.ts` (Task 10) · `renderDocument(page: DraftPage, ctx: RenderContext): string`, `renderStylesheet(ctx: RenderContext): string` (Task 13)
- Produces: `renderBundle(draft: SiteDraft, ctx: RenderContext): SiteBundle`

Task 15's `fixtureDeployer` and Task 16's `cloudflareDeployer` both take exactly the `SiteBundle` this produces. Task 18 re-exports `renderBundle` as the **only** supported way to turn a draft into HTML — that is what keeps the escaping gate unbypassable, since every path to markup runs through the renderers this function calls.

`renderStylesheet` and the inlined `tokens.css` do not duplicate each other: `<head>` inlines tokens plus the theme custom properties because they must resolve before first paint, and `styles.css` carries layout only.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`packages/sites/src/render/bundle.test.ts`:

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { DraftPage, SiteDraft } from '../normalize/draft'
import type { NormalizedSection } from '../normalize/section-content'
import type { RenderContext } from './context'
import { renderBundle } from './bundle'

/** noUncheckedIndexedAccess is on: index once, loudly, instead of sprinkling `!`.
 *  The same helper Task 6's to-rows.test.ts defines — a bare `files[0].content` is a
 *  compile error under `tsc --noEmit`, and test files are typechecked. */
const at = <T>(list: readonly T[], index: number): T => {
  const value = list[index]
  if (value === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`expected an element at index ${index}, got a list of length ${list.length}`)
  }
  return value
}

const buildCtx = (overrides: Partial<RenderContext> = {}): RenderContext => ({
  siteName: 'Sharma Dental',
  tokensCss: ':root{--ink:#131313}',
  theme: null,
  formAction: '/api/leads/sharma-dental',
  canonicalOrigin: null,
  ...overrides,
})

const hero = (headline: string): NormalizedSection => ({
  section: { kind: 'hero', content: { headline } },
  sort: 0,
  raw: { headline },
})

const buildPage = (overrides: Partial<DraftPage> = {}): DraftPage => ({
  path: '/',
  title: 'Home',
  seoDescription: 'Painless dentistry.',
  sort: 0,
  sections: [hero('Smile brighter')],
  ...overrides,
})

const buildDraft = (overrides: Partial<SiteDraft> = {}): SiteDraft => ({
  name: 'Sharma Dental',
  goal: 'leads',
  pages: [buildPage(), buildPage({ path: '/about', title: 'About', sort: 1 })],
  ...overrides,
})

const pathsOf = (draft: SiteDraft, ctx: RenderContext): string[] =>
  renderBundle(draft, ctx).files.map((file) => file.path)

describe('renderBundle — file layout', () => {
  it('emits one html file per page at its pathToFile location, plus one stylesheet', () => {
    expect(pathsOf(buildDraft(), buildCtx())).toEqual(['index.html', 'about/index.html', 'styles.css'])
  })

  it('maps the root page to index.html so the deploy target serves it at /', () => {
    const paths = pathsOf(buildDraft({ pages: [buildPage({ path: '/' })] }), buildCtx())

    expect(paths).toEqual(['index.html', 'styles.css'])
  })

  it('labels html and css with charset-bearing content types so non-ASCII copy renders correctly', () => {
    const { files } = renderBundle(buildDraft(), buildCtx())

    expect(at(files, 0).contentType).toBe('text/html; charset=utf-8')
    expect(at(files, files.length - 1).contentType).toBe('text/css; charset=utf-8')
  })

  it('puts the rendered document in each page file', () => {
    const { files } = renderBundle(buildDraft({ pages: [buildPage()] }), buildCtx())

    expect(at(files, 0).content).toContain('<h1>Smile brighter</h1>')
    expect(at(files, 0).content).toContain('<html lang="en">')
  })

  it('puts the layout stylesheet in styles.css', () => {
    const { files } = renderBundle(buildDraft(), buildCtx())
    const stylesheet = files.find((file) => file.path === 'styles.css')

    expect(stylesheet?.content).toContain('--site-font-body')
  })

  it('emits only the stylesheet when the draft has no pages, rather than throwing', () => {
    expect(pathsOf(buildDraft({ pages: [] }), buildCtx())).toEqual(['styles.css'])
  })
})

describe('renderBundle — bundleId is a content hash (the deploy identity)', () => {
  it('is identical for two structurally identical drafts, so a redeploy of unchanged content is detectable', () => {
    const ctx = buildCtx()

    expect(renderBundle(buildDraft(), ctx).bundleId).toBe(renderBundle(buildDraft(), ctx).bundleId)
  })

  it('changes when a single headline changes, so a real edit is never mistaken for a no-op', () => {
    const ctx = buildCtx()
    const before = renderBundle(buildDraft({ pages: [buildPage()] }), ctx).bundleId
    const after = renderBundle(
      buildDraft({ pages: [buildPage({ sections: [hero('Smile wider')] })] }),
      ctx,
    ).bundleId

    expect(after).not.toBe(before)
  })

  it('changes when the theme changes, because the emitted CSS differs', () => {
    const before = renderBundle(buildDraft(), buildCtx({ theme: null })).bundleId
    const after = renderBundle(buildDraft(), buildCtx({ tokensCss: ':root{--ink:#000000}' })).bundleId

    expect(after).not.toBe(before)
  })

  it('changes when a page is added, so a new route forces a fresh deploy', () => {
    const ctx = buildCtx()
    const before = renderBundle(buildDraft({ pages: [buildPage()] }), ctx).bundleId
    const after = renderBundle(buildDraft(), ctx).bundleId

    expect(after).not.toBe(before)
  })

  it('is independent of page ordering, because the file set is what gets served', () => {
    const ctx = buildCtx()
    const forward = buildDraft()
    const reversed = buildDraft({ pages: [...forward.pages].reverse() })

    expect(renderBundle(reversed, ctx).bundleId).toBe(renderBundle(forward, ctx).bundleId)
  })

  it('is a 64-character lowercase hex sha256 digest', () => {
    expect(renderBundle(buildDraft(), buildCtx()).bundleId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`packages/sites/src/render/bundle.ts`:

```typescript
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
import type { SiteDraft } from '../normalize/draft'
import { pathToFile } from '../normalize/path'
import type { BundleFile, SiteBundle } from '../deploy/port'
import type { RenderContext } from './context'
import { renderDocument, renderStylesheet } from './document'

const STYLESHEET_FILE = 'styles.css'
const HTML_CONTENT_TYPE = 'text/html; charset=utf-8'
const CSS_CONTENT_TYPE = 'text/css; charset=utf-8'
const HASH_ALGORITHM = 'sha256'
/** NUL/SOH cannot appear in a path, a content type, or escaped HTML — so no ambiguity. */
const FIELD_SEPARATOR = '\u0000'
const RECORD_SEPARATOR = '\u0001'

/**
 * Sorting the joined records makes the digest a function of the file SET, not the order
 * the pages happened to arrive in — reordering pages must not force a pointless redeploy.
 */
const stableSerialize = (files: BundleFile[]): string =>
  files
    .map((file) => [file.path, file.contentType, file.content].join(FIELD_SEPARATOR))
    .sort()
    .join(RECORD_SEPARATOR)

const hashFiles = (files: BundleFile[]): string =>
  createHash(HASH_ALGORITHM).update(stableSerialize(files), 'utf8').digest('hex')

export const renderBundle = (draft: SiteDraft, ctx: RenderContext): SiteBundle => {
  const pages: BundleFile[] = draft.pages.map((page) => ({
    path: pathToFile(page.path),
    content: renderDocument(page, ctx),
    contentType: HTML_CONTENT_TYPE,
  }))

  const stylesheet: BundleFile = {
    path: STYLESHEET_FILE,
    content: renderStylesheet(ctx),
    contentType: CSS_CONTENT_TYPE,
  }

  const files = [...pages, stylesheet]
  return { files, bundleId: hashFiles(files) }
}
```

### Task 15: Fixture deployer (`src/deploy/fixture.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/sites/src/deploy/fixture.ts`
- Test: `packages/sites/src/deploy/fixture.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `appendHistory`, `SiteDeployStateSchema`, `type Deployer`, `type DeployContext`, `type SiteBundle`, `type SiteDeployState`, `type SiteDeployHistoryEntry` — all from `./port` (Task 10) · `ok`, `err`, `appError`, `type Result`, `type AppError` from `@sahoda/shared`. The bundles it writes are produced by Task 14.
- Produces:
  - `export type WriteBundleFile = (path: string, content: string) => Promise<void>`
  - `export interface FixtureDeployerDeps { outDir: string; writeFile: WriteBundleFile; now?: () => Date }`
  - `export const createFixtureDeployer = (deps: FixtureDeployerDeps): Deployer`

**Why this is the DEFAULT deployer.** *No fake links, ever* is repeated in four canon docs. Until Cloudflare credentials exist, the honest answer to "where is my site?" is a local directory and a `file://` URL labelled `preview: true` — never a plausible `https://<slug>.sahoda.site` that resolves to nothing. `ctx.baseDomain` is deliberately unread in this file, so the fabrication is not merely avoided, it is unavailable: there is no expression in the module that could produce a public hostname even by accident, and a test asserts the base domain never appears in the returned URL.

`writeFile` is injected so the package never imports `node:fs` — the whole suite runs against a recording closure with no filesystem involved.

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { AppError, Result } from '@sahoda/shared'
import { createFixtureDeployer, type WriteBundleFile } from './fixture'
import {
  SiteDeployStateSchema,
  type DeployContext,
  type SiteBundle,
  type SiteDeployHistoryEntry,
  type SiteDeployState,
} from './port'

const FIXED_NOW = new Date('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
const OUT_DIR = '/tmp/sahoda-preview'
const BASE_DOMAIN = 'sahoda.site'

function bundle(overrides: Partial<SiteBundle> = {}): SiteBundle {
  return {
    bundleId: 'bundle-abc123',
    files: [
      {
        path: 'index.html',
        content: '<!doctype html><title>Acme Chai</title>',
        contentType: 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
      },
      {
        path: 'about/index.html',
        content: '<!doctype html><title>About Acme Chai</title>',
        contentType: 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
      },
      {
        path: 'styles.css',
        content: ':root{--p:oklch(0.55 0.14 250)}',
        contentType: 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
      },
    ],
    ...overrides,
  }
}

function context(overrides: Partial<DeployContext> = {}): DeployContext {
  return {
    workspaceId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
    siteId: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222',
    slug: 'acme-chai',
    baseDomain: BASE_DOMAIN,
    traceId: 'trace-fixture-1',
    previous: [],
    now: () => FIXED_NOW,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

/** Records every (path, content) pair the deployer wrote. No mocking library. */
function recordingWriter(): { writeFile: WriteBundleFile; calls: () => Array<[string, string]> } {
  const calls: Array<[string, string]> = []
  return {
    writeFile: async (path, content) => {
      calls.push([path, content])
    },
    calls: () => calls,
  }
}

function unwrap(result: Result<SiteDeployState>): SiteDeployState {
  if (!result.ok) {
    throw new Error(`expected ok, got ${result.error.code}: ${result.error.message}`)
  }
  return result.data
}

function unwrapErr(result: Result<SiteDeployState>): AppError {
  if (result.ok) {
    throw new Error('expected an error result, got ok')
  }
  return result.error
}

function historyEntry(bundleId: string): SiteDeployHistoryEntry {
  return { bundleId, deployedAt: '2026-07-18T00:00:00.000Z', url: null, preview: true }
}

describe('fixtureDeployer — the honest default: a local preview, never a public URL', () => {
  it('always reports preview true, because this is not a reachable site', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.preview).toBe(true)
  })

  it('reports status live and deployer fixture, so the UI can label it "preview"', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.status).toBe('live')
    expect(state.deployer).toBe('fixture')
  })

  it('returns a file:// url pointing at the written index.html', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.url).toBe('file:///tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/index.html')
  })

  it('NEVER emits an https url — a plausible public link is the exact v1 sin this forbids', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.url).not.toContain('https://')
    expect(state.url).not.toContain('http://')
  })

  it('NEVER embeds ctx.baseDomain in the url, so a refactor cannot start faking sahoda.site', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ baseDomain: BASE_DOMAIN })))

    expect(state.url).not.toContain(BASE_DOMAIN)
    expect(state.url).not.toContain('acme-chai.')
  })

  it('leaves scriptName and error null, since no worker exists on the fixture path', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.scriptName).toBeNull()
    expect(state.error).toBeNull()
  })

  it('writes EVERY bundle file through the injected writer, under outDir/slug', async () => {
    const writer = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile: writer.writeFile })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(writer.calls()).toEqual([
      ['/tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/index.html', '<!doctype html><title>Acme Chai</title>'],
      [
        '/tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/about/index.html',
        '<!doctype html><title>About Acme Chai</title>',
      ],
      ['/tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/styles.css', ':root{--p:oklch(0.55 0.14 250)}'],
    ])
  })

  it('normalizes a duplicated slash between outDir and the file path', async () => {
    const writer = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: '/tmp/sahoda-preview/', writeFile: writer.writeFile })

    await deploy(bundle({ files: [{ path: '/index.html', content: 'x', contentType: 'text/html' }] }), context())

    expect(writer.calls()[0]?.[0]).toBe('/tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/index.html')
  })

  it('returns a typed PROVIDER_ERROR when the writer rejects, instead of throwing at the caller', async () => {
    const writeFile: WriteBundleFile = async () => {
      throw new Error('EACCES: permission denied, open /tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/index.html')
    }
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())
    const error = unwrapErr(result)

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(error.code).toBe('PROVIDER_ERROR')
    expect(error.traceId).toBe('trace-fixture-1')
  })

  it('carries only the error NAME in details, never the filesystem error text', async () => {
    const writeFile: WriteBundleFile = async () => {
      throw new Error('EACCES: permission denied, open /home/secret/path')
    }
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.details).toEqual({ name: 'Error', path: 'index.html' })
    expect(JSON.stringify(error)).not.toContain('/home/secret/path')
  })

  it('stops at the first failing file rather than writing a half-broken preview', async () => {
    const written: string[] = []
    const writeFile: WriteBundleFile = async (path) => {
      if (path.endsWith('about/index.html')) throw new Error('ENOSPC')
      written.push(path)
    }
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(written).toEqual(['/tmp/sahoda-preview/acme-chai/index.html'])
  })

  it('uses the injected clock so deployedAt is deterministic, not wall-clock', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.deployedAt).toBe('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
  })

  it('prefers ctx.now over deps.now, so a per-deploy clock wins over the factory default', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({
      outDir: OUT_DIR,
      writeFile,
      now: () => new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ now: () => FIXED_NOW })))

    expect(state.deployedAt).toBe('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
  })

  it('prepends this deploy to history and evicts the oldest of five previous entries', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })
    const previous = ['b5', 'b4', 'b3', 'b2', 'b1'].map(historyEntry)

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ previous })))

    expect(state.history.map((h) => h.bundleId)).toEqual(['bundle-abc123', 'b5', 'b4', 'b3', 'b2'])
    expect(state.history[0]?.preview).toBe(true)
    expect(state.history[0]?.deployedAt).toBe('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
  })

  it('produces a state that parses against SiteDeployStateSchema, so the jsonb write is safe', async () => {
    const { writeFile } = recordingWriter()
    const deploy = createFixtureDeployer({ outDir: OUT_DIR, writeFile })
    const previous = ['b5', 'b4', 'b3', 'b2', 'b1'].map(historyEntry)

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ previous })))

    expect(SiteDeployStateSchema.parse(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)))).toEqual(state)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`packages/sites/src/deploy/fixture.ts`

```typescript
import { appError, err, ok } from '@sahoda/shared'
import type { Result } from '@sahoda/shared'
import {
  appendHistory,
  type DeployContext,
  type Deployer,
  type SiteBundle,
  type SiteDeployState,
} from './port'

const DEPLOYER_NAME = 'fixture' as const
const ENTRY_FILE = 'index.html'
const FILE_URL_SCHEME = 'file://'

/** Writes one bundle file. Injected so the package never imports `node:fs`. */
export type WriteBundleFile = (path: string, content: string) => Promise<void>

export interface FixtureDeployerDeps {
  outDir: string
  writeFile: WriteBundleFile
  now?: () => Date
}

/** Join path segments with exactly one separator, preserving a leading slash on the root. */
const joinPath = (...parts: string[]): string =>
  parts
    .map((part, index) => (index === 0 ? part.replace(/\/+$/, '') : part.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')))
    .filter((part) => part.length > 0)
    .join('/')

/**
 * The DEFAULT deployer. It writes the bundle to a local directory and reports
 * `preview: true` with a `file://` URL. It NEVER claims a public URL — "no fake links,
 * ever" is repeated in four canon docs and is the load-bearing constraint of this feature.
 * `ctx.baseDomain` is deliberately unread here: this deployer has no way to fabricate
 * a `https://<slug>.<domain>` address even by accident.
 */
export const createFixtureDeployer = (deps: FixtureDeployerDeps): Deployer => {
  return async (bundle: SiteBundle, ctx: DeployContext): Promise<Result<SiteDeployState>> => {
    const now = ctx.now ?? deps.now ?? (() => new Date())
    const siteDir = joinPath(deps.outDir, ctx.slug)

    for (const file of bundle.files) {
      try {
        await deps.writeFile(joinPath(siteDir, file.path), file.content)
      } catch (error) {
        // Stop at the first failure: a half-written directory served as a "preview" would
        // be its own dishonesty. Carry only the error NAME — dependency error text can
        // embed absolute host paths and is persisted into sites.deploy.
        return err(
          appError('PROVIDER_ERROR', 'Could not write the preview bundle to disk.', ctx.traceId, {
            name: error instanceof Error ? error.name : 'unknown',
            path: file.path,
          }),
        )
      }
    }

    const deployedAt = now().toISOString()
    const url = `${FILE_URL_SCHEME}${joinPath(siteDir, ENTRY_FILE)}`

    return ok({
      deployer: DEPLOYER_NAME,
      status: 'live',
      preview: true,
      url,
      bundleId: bundle.bundleId,
      scriptName: null,
      deployedAt,
      error: null,
      history: appendHistory(ctx.previous, {
        bundleId: bundle.bundleId,
        deployedAt,
        url,
        preview: true,
      }),
    })
  }
}
```

```bash
the error name and the bundle-relative path — never the host path."
```

### Task 16: Transport port + Cloudflare deployer

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/sites/src/transport.ts`
- Create: `packages/sites/src/deploy/cloudflare.ts`
- Create: `packages/sites/fixtures/cloudflare/upload.success.json`
- Create: `packages/sites/fixtures/cloudflare/upload.unauthorized.json`
- Create: `packages/sites/fixtures/cloudflare/upload.rate-limited.json`
- Create: `packages/sites/fixtures/cloudflare/upload.server-error.json`
- Create: `packages/sites/fixtures/cloudflare/upload.malformed.json`
- Test: `packages/sites/src/transport.test.ts`
- Test: `packages/sites/src/deploy/cloudflare.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (from Task 10): `appendHistory`, `type Deployer`, `type DeployContext`, `type SiteBundle`, `type BundleFile`, `type SiteDeployState`, `SiteDeployStateSchema` — all from `./port`. Plus `ok`, `err`, `appError`, `type Result` from `@sahoda/shared`.
- Produces:
  - `export interface TransportRequest { method: string; url: string; headers?: Record<string, string>; body?: string }`
  - `export interface TransportResponse { status: number; headers: Record<string, string>; body: string }`
  - `export type Transport = (req: TransportRequest) => Promise<TransportResponse>`
  - `export const fixtureTransport = (recorded: TransportResponse): Transport`
  - `export const routedTransport = (routes: Array<{ method?: string; urlIncludes: string; response: TransportResponse }>): Transport`
  - `export const fetchTransport = (fetchImpl?: typeof fetch): Transport`
  - `export interface CloudflareConfig { apiToken: string; accountId: string; dispatchNamespace: string; baseDomain: string }`
  - `export interface CloudflareDeployerDeps { config: CloudflareConfig | null; transport: Transport; now?: () => Date }`
  - `export const createCloudflareDeployer = (deps: CloudflareDeployerDeps): Deployer`

**Notes for the implementer:** `TransportResponse.body` is a **string**, so the recorded JSON fixtures store the body as a JSON-encoded string, not a nested object. That means a fixture file is byte-identical to what the wire returned, and it imports directly as a `TransportResponse` with no cast. No module in this package ever calls `fetch` directly.

`packages/sites/src/transport.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import {
  fetchTransport,
  fixtureTransport,
  routedTransport,
  type TransportRequest,
  type TransportResponse,
} from './transport'

const OK_RES: TransportResponse = {
  status: 200,
  headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
  body: '{"success":true}',
}

const CREATED_RES: TransportResponse = {
  status: 201,
  headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
  body: '{"success":true,"result":{"id":"site-acme-chai"}}',
}

describe('fixtureTransport — replay one recorded response', () => {
  it('returns the recorded status, headers and body for any request', async () => {
    const transport = fixtureTransport(OK_RES)

    const res = await transport({ method: 'PUT', url: 'https://api.cloudflare.com/anything' })

    expect(res.status).toBe(200)
    expect(res.headers).toEqual({ 'content-type': 'application/json' })
    expect(JSON.parse(res.body)).toEqual({ success: true })
  })

  it('ignores the request entirely, so a single-call adapter needs no routing setup', async () => {
    const transport = fixtureTransport(CREATED_RES)

    const first = await transport({ method: 'GET', url: 'https://a.example' })
    const second = await transport({ method: 'DELETE', url: 'https://b.example' })

    expect(first).toEqual(second)
  })
})

describe('routedTransport — per-call fixture routing for multi-call flows', () => {
  it('routes each request to the fixture whose method AND url substring match', async () => {
    const transport = routedTransport([
      { method: 'PUT', urlIncludes: '/scripts/', response: CREATED_RES },
      { method: 'GET', urlIncludes: '/namespaces', response: OK_RES },
    ])

    const upload = await transport({ method: 'PUT', url: 'https://api.example/scripts/site-a' })
    const list = await transport({ method: 'GET', url: 'https://api.example/namespaces' })

    expect(upload.status).toBe(201)
    expect(list.status).toBe(200)
  })

  it('matches on url substring alone when method is omitted', async () => {
    const transport = routedTransport([{ urlIncludes: 'namespaces', response: OK_RES }])

    const res = await transport({ method: 'DELETE', url: 'https://api.example/v4/namespaces/x' })

    expect(res.status).toBe(200)
  })

  it('THROWS on an unmatched url, so a missing fixture fails loudly not confusingly', async () => {
    const transport = routedTransport([{ urlIncludes: '/scripts/', response: CREATED_RES }])

    await expect(
      transport({ method: 'GET', url: 'https://api.example/unexpected' }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/no fixture route matched GET https:\/\/api\.example\/unexpected/)
  })

  it('THROWS when the url matches but the method does not, catching a wrong-verb bug', async () => {
    const transport = routedTransport([
      { method: 'PUT', urlIncludes: '/scripts/', response: CREATED_RES },
    ])

    await expect(
      transport({ method: 'POST', url: 'https://api.example/scripts/site-a' }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/no fixture route/)
  })

  it('uses the FIRST matching route when several could apply, making order meaningful', async () => {
    const transport = routedTransport([
      { urlIncludes: 'api', response: OK_RES },
      { urlIncludes: 'api', response: CREATED_RES },
    ])

    const res = await transport({ method: 'GET', url: 'https://api.example/x' })

    expect(res.status).toBe(200)
  })
})

describe('fetchTransport — the only place a real network call is made', () => {
  it('forwards method, url, headers and body to the injected fetch', async () => {
    let seenUrl = ''
    let seenInit: RequestInit | undefined
    const fakeFetch = (async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
      seenUrl = url
      seenInit = init
      return new Response('{}', { status: 200 })
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch
    const transport = fetchTransport(fakeFetch)

    const req: TransportRequest = {
      method: 'PUT',
      url: 'https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/acct/workers',
      headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer t' },
      body: 'payload',
    }
    await transport(req)

    expect(seenUrl).toBe('https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/acct/workers')
    expect(seenInit?.method).toBe('PUT')
    expect(seenInit?.headers).toEqual({ Authorization: 'Bearer t' })
    expect(seenInit?.body).toBe('payload')
  })

  it('flattens the response headers into a plain record and returns the body as text', async () => {
    const fakeFetch = (async () =>
      new Response('{"success":true}', {
        status: 201,
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'cf-ray': 'abc' },
      })) as unknown as typeof fetch
    const transport = fetchTransport(fakeFetch)

    const res = await transport({ method: 'GET', url: 'https://api.example' })

    expect(res.status).toBe(201)
    expect(res.headers['content-type']).toBe('application/json')
    expect(res.headers['cf-ray']).toBe('abc')
    expect(res.body).toBe('{"success":true}')
  })
})
```

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
import type { AppError, Result } from '@sahoda/shared'
import { createCloudflareDeployer, type CloudflareConfig } from './cloudflare'
import {
  SiteDeployStateSchema,
  type DeployContext,
  type SiteBundle,
  type SiteDeployHistoryEntry,
  type SiteDeployState,
} from './port'
import { fixtureTransport, type Transport, type TransportRequest } from '../transport'
import uploadSuccess from '../../fixtures/cloudflare/upload.success.json'
import uploadUnauthorized from '../../fixtures/cloudflare/upload.unauthorized.json'
import uploadRateLimited from '../../fixtures/cloudflare/upload.rate-limited.json'
import uploadServerError from '../../fixtures/cloudflare/upload.server-error.json'
import uploadMalformed from '../../fixtures/cloudflare/upload.malformed.json'

const FIXED_NOW = new Date('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
const API_TOKEN = 'cf-secret-token-123'

function config(overrides: Partial<CloudflareConfig> = {}): CloudflareConfig {
  return {
    apiToken: API_TOKEN,
    accountId: 'acct-9f2',
    dispatchNamespace: 'sahoda-sites',
    baseDomain: 'sahoda.site',
    ...overrides,
  }
}

function bundle(overrides: Partial<SiteBundle> = {}): SiteBundle {
  return {
    bundleId: 'bundle-abc123',
    files: [
      {
        path: 'index.html',
        content: '<!doctype html><title>Acme Chai</title>',
        contentType: 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
      },
      {
        path: 'styles.css',
        content: ':root{--p:oklch(0.55 0.14 250)}',
        contentType: 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
      },
    ],
    ...overrides,
  }
}

function context(overrides: Partial<DeployContext> = {}): DeployContext {
  return {
    workspaceId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
    siteId: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222',
    slug: 'acme-chai',
    baseDomain: null,
    traceId: 'trace-cf-1',
    previous: [],
    now: () => FIXED_NOW,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

/** Wrap a fixture transport but record the last outbound request for assertions. */
function capturing(recorded: typeof uploadSuccess): {
  transport: Transport
  last: () => TransportRequest | undefined
} {
  const inner = fixtureTransport(recorded)
  let last: TransportRequest | undefined
  const transport: Transport = async (req) => {
    last = req
    return inner(req)
  }
  return { transport, last: () => last }
}

function unwrap(result: Result<SiteDeployState>): SiteDeployState {
  if (!result.ok) {
    throw new Error(`expected ok, got ${result.error.code}: ${result.error.message}`)
  }
  return result.data
}

function unwrapErr(result: Result<SiteDeployState>): AppError {
  if (result.ok) {
    throw new Error('expected an error result, got ok')
  }
  return result.error
}

function historyEntry(bundleId: string): SiteDeployHistoryEntry {
  return {
    bundleId,
    deployedAt: '2026-07-18T00:00:00.000Z',
    url: 'https://acme-chai.sahoda.site',
    preview: false,
  }
}

describe('cloudflareDeployer — unconfigured is a typed error, never a fake success', () => {
  it('returns PROVIDER_ERROR cloudflare-not-configured when config is null', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: null, transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess) })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.code).toBe('PROVIDER_ERROR')
    expect(error.message).toBe('cloudflare-not-configured')
    expect(error.traceId).toBe('trace-cf-1')
  })

  it('attempts NO network call when config is null — credentials gate the wire, not the response', async () => {
    let called = false
    const transport: Transport = async (req) => {
      called = true
      return fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess)(req)
    }
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: null, transport })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(called).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('cloudflareDeployer — successful upload against a recorded fixture', () => {
  it('reports preview false and status live, the only path allowed to claim a public URL', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.preview).toBe(false)
    expect(state.status).toBe('live')
    expect(state.deployer).toBe('cloudflare')
  })

  it('builds the url from the INJECTED base domain, never a hardcoded one', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.url).toBe('https://acme-chai.sahoda.site')
  })

  it('lets ctx.baseDomain override config.baseDomain, so a staging domain is one field', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ baseDomain: 'sahoda-staging.site' })))

    expect(state.url).toBe('https://acme-chai.sahoda-staging.site')
  })

  it('records the worker script name, the bundle id and a deterministic deployedAt', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(state.scriptName).toBe('site-acme-chai')
    expect(state.bundleId).toBe('bundle-abc123')
    expect(state.deployedAt).toBe('2026-07-19T09:30:00.000Z')
    expect(state.error).toBeNull()
  })

  it('prepends the deploy to history and evicts the oldest of five previous entries', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })
    const previous = ['b5', 'b4', 'b3', 'b2', 'b1'].map(historyEntry)

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ previous })))

    expect(state.history.map((h) => h.bundleId)).toEqual(['bundle-abc123', 'b5', 'b4', 'b3', 'b2'])
    expect(state.history[0]?.preview).toBe(false)
  })

  it('produces a state that parses against SiteDeployStateSchema for the jsonb write', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadSuccess),
    })

    const state = unwrap(await deploy(bundle(), context({ previous: ['b1'].map(historyEntry) })))

    expect(SiteDeployStateSchema.parse(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)))).toEqual(state)
  })
})

describe('cloudflareDeployer — the outbound Workers-for-Platforms request', () => {
  it('PUTs to the dispatch-namespace script endpoint for this account and slug', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(last()?.method).toBe('PUT')
    expect(last()?.url).toBe(
      'https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/acct-9f2/workers/dispatch/namespaces/sahoda-sites/scripts/site-acme-chai',
    )
  })

  it('sends bearer auth and a multipart content-type carrying the declared boundary', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(last()?.headers?.['Authorization']).toBe('Bearer cf-secret-token-123')
    const contentType = last()?.headers?.['content-type'] ?? ''
    expect(contentType).toContain('multipart/form-data; boundary=')
    const boundary = contentType.split('boundary=')[1] ?? ''
    expect(boundary.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(last()?.body).toContain(`--${boundary}`)
    expect(last()?.body?.endsWith(`--${boundary}--\r\n`)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('uploads a module worker whose metadata names the entry module', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(last()?.body).toContain('"main_module":"worker.js"')
    expect(last()?.body).toContain('Content-Type: application/javascript+module')
  })

  it('embeds every bundle file in the worker, so the deployed site serves all pages', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    const body = last()?.body ?? ''
    expect(body).toContain(JSON.stringify('<!doctype html><title>Acme Chai</title>').slice(1, -1))
    expect(body).toContain('styles.css')
    expect(body).toContain('text/css; charset=utf-8')
  })

  it('escapes U+2028 in embedded copy, which would otherwise break the worker module', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(
      bundle({
        files: [
          { path: 'index.html', content: 'line\u2028break', contentType: 'text/html' },
        ],
      }),
      context(),
    )

    const body = last()?.body ?? ''
    expect(body).not.toContain('\u2028')
    expect(body).toContain('\\u2028')
  })
})

const HTTP_FAILURES = [
  { name: '401 unauthorized', fixture: uploadUnauthorized, code: 'PROVIDER_ERROR', status: 401 },
  { name: '429 rate limited', fixture: uploadRateLimited, code: 'RATE_LIMITED', status: 429 },
  { name: '500 server error', fixture: uploadServerError, code: 'PROVIDER_ERROR', status: 500 },
] as const

describe('cloudflareDeployer — HTTP failures become typed errors, never a claimed URL', () => {
  for (const testCase of HTTP_FAILURES) {
    it(`maps ${testCase.name} to ${testCase.code} without returning ok`, async () => {
      const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
        config: config(),
        transport: fixtureTransport(testCase.fixture),
      })

      const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())
      const error = unwrapErr(result)

      expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
      expect(error.code).toBe(testCase.code)
      expect(error.message).toBe(`Cloudflare rejected the upload with HTTP ${testCase.status}.`)
      expect(error.traceId).toBe('trace-cf-1')
    })
  }

  it('carries ONLY status and the provider\'s own message in details on an HTTP error', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadUnauthorized),
    })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.details).toEqual({ status: 401, detail: 'Authentication error' })
  })

  it('omits detail when the error body is not the expected Cloudflare envelope', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport({ status: 502, headers: {}, body: '<html>bad gateway</html>' }),
    })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.details).toEqual({ status: 502, detail: undefined })
  })

  it('truncates a very long provider message to 300 chars so jsonb cannot be flooded', async () => {
    const long = 'x'.repeat(1000)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport({
        status: 400,
        headers: {},
        body: JSON.stringify({ success: false, errors: [{ code: 10001, message: long }] }),
      }),
    })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect((error.details as { detail: string }).detail).toHaveLength(300)
  })

  it('rejects a 2xx with malformed JSON rather than reporting a site that may not exist', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadMalformed),
    })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())
    const error = unwrapErr(result)

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(error.code).toBe('PROVIDER_ERROR')
    expect(error.message).toBe('Cloudflare returned a success status without a usable result.')
    expect(error.details).toEqual({ status: 200, bodySample: '{"success":true,"result":' })
  })

  it('rejects a 2xx whose envelope reports success:false', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport({
        status: 200,
        headers: {},
        body: '{"success":false,"errors":[],"result":null}',
      }),
    })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.code).toBe('PROVIDER_ERROR')
    expect(error.details).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      bodySample: '{"success":false,"errors":[],"result":null}',
    })
  })
})

describe('cloudflareDeployer — leak invariants', () => {
  it('carries only the error NAME when the transport throws before a response arrives', async () => {
    const transport: Transport = async () => {
      throw new TypeError('fetch failed')
    }
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    const error = unwrapErr(await deploy(bundle(), context()))

    expect(error.code).toBe('PROVIDER_ERROR')
    expect(error.message).toBe('Cloudflare upload failed before a response was received.')
    expect(error.details).toEqual({ name: 'TypeError' })
  })

  it('SECRET LEAK: a transport error echoing the Authorization header never reaches the Result', async () => {
    const transport: Transport = async () => {
      // A misbehaving transport that embeds the outbound Authorization header in its message.
      throw new Error(
        'PUT /workers/dispatch failed — Authorization: Bearer cf-secret-token-123',
      )
    }
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('cf-secret-token-123')
    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('Bearer')
  })

  it('SECRET LEAK: the api token never reaches the Result on an HTTP failure path either', async () => {
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({
      config: config(),
      transport: fixtureTransport(uploadUnauthorized),
    })

    const result = await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('cf-secret-token-123')
  })

  it('SECRET LEAK: the api token is never written into the request body, only the header', async () => {
    const { transport, last } = capturing(uploadSuccess)
    const deploy = createCloudflareDeployer({ config: config(), transport })

    await deploy(bundle(), context())

    expect(last()?.body).not.toContain('cf-secret-token-123')
  })
})
```

`packages/sites/src/transport.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * A minimal fetch-shaped HTTP port, ported from `packages/publishing/src/transport.ts`.
 * Deployers depend on this instead of calling the network directly, so tests replay
 * RECORDED fixtures and never touch live Cloudflare. Production wires {@link fetchTransport}.
 * No other module in this package may call `fetch`.
 */
export interface TransportRequest {
  method: string
  url: string
  headers?: Record<string, string>
  /** Site bundles are text (HTML/CSS/JS), so a string body covers every call here. */
  body?: string
}

export interface TransportResponse {
  status: number
  headers: Record<string, string>
  /** Raw response body text; deployers JSON-parse as needed. */
  body: string
}

export type Transport = (req: TransportRequest) => Promise<TransportResponse>

/**
 * Replay a single recorded response regardless of the request — the backbone of the
 * deployer fixture tests. Recorded fixtures in `./fixtures/**` are stored in exactly
 * this shape, body included, so a fixture is byte-identical to the wire response.
 */
export const fixtureTransport = (recorded: TransportResponse): Transport => {
  return async () => ({
    status: recorded.status,
    headers: { ...recorded.headers },
    body: recorded.body,
  })
}

/** A single route for {@link routedTransport}: match by method and/or URL substring. */
export interface FixtureRoute {
  method?: string
  urlIncludes: string
  response: TransportResponse
}

/**
 * Route each request to a recorded response — required for multi-call flows where the
 * blind single-response {@link fixtureTransport} would silently replay the wrong fixture.
 * An unmatched request THROWS so a missing fixture fails loudly, never confusingly.
 */
export const routedTransport = (routes: FixtureRoute[]): Transport => {
  return async (req) => {
    const route = routes.find(
      (candidate) =>
        (candidate.method === undefined || candidate.method === req.method) &&
        req.url.includes(candidate.urlIncludes),
    )
    if (!route) {
      throw new Error(`no fixture route matched ${req.method} ${req.url}`)
    }
    return fixtureTransport(route.response)(req)
  }
}

/** Production transport backed by the platform's global `fetch`. */
export const fetchTransport = (fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch): Transport => {
  return async (req) => {
    const res = await fetchImpl(req.url, {
      method: req.method,
      headers: req.headers,
      body: req.body,
    })
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {}
    res.headers.forEach((value, key) => {
      headers[key] = value
    })
    return { status: res.status, headers, body: await res.text() }
  }
}
```

`packages/sites/src/deploy/cloudflare.ts`

```typescript
import { appError, err, ok } from '@sahoda/shared'
import type { ErrorCode, Result } from '@sahoda/shared'
import type { Transport, TransportResponse } from '../transport'
import {
  appendHistory,
  type BundleFile,
  type DeployContext,
  type Deployer,
  type SiteBundle,
  type SiteDeployState,
} from './port'

const DEPLOYER_NAME = 'cloudflare' as const
const CF_API_ROOT = 'https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4'
const SCRIPT_NAME_PREFIX = 'site-'
const WORKER_ENTRY = 'worker.js'
const COMPATIBILITY_DATE = '2026-01-01'
const MULTIPART_BOUNDARY = 'sahoda-sites-boundary-0f6c1c'
const CRLF = '\r\n'
const DETAIL_MAX = 300
const NOT_CONFIGURED = 'cloudflare-not-configured'
const ENTRY_FILE = 'index.html'
/** U+2028/U+2029 terminate a line inside a JS module and would break the worker source. */
const LINE_SEPARATORS = /[\u2028\u2029]/g

export interface CloudflareConfig {
  apiToken: string
  accountId: string
  dispatchNamespace: string
  baseDomain: string
}

export interface CloudflareDeployerDeps {
  config: CloudflareConfig | null
  transport: Transport
  now?: () => Date
}

/** Bundle-relative asset key: never leading-slashed, so lookups are unambiguous. */
const assetKey = (path: string): string => path.replace(/^\/+/, '')

/** Serialize the bundle into a module worker that serves it from an in-script asset map. */
const workerSource = (files: BundleFile[]): string => {
  const assets: Record<string, { body: string; contentType: string }> = {}
  for (const file of files) {
    assets[assetKey(file.path)] = { body: file.content, contentType: file.contentType }
  }
  const literal = JSON.stringify(assets).replace(LINE_SEPARATORS, (char) =>
    char === '\u2028' ? '\\u2028' : '\\u2029',
  )
  return [
    `const ASSETS = ${literal};`,
    `const ENTRY = ${JSON.stringify(ENTRY_FILE)};`,
    'export default {',
    '  fetch(request) {',
    '    const path = new URL(request.url).pathname.replace(/^\\/+/, "");',
    '    const asset = ASSETS[path] || ASSETS[path ? path + "/" + ENTRY : ENTRY];',
    '    if (!asset) {',
    '      return new Response("Not found", { status: 404, headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" } });',
    '    }',
    '    return new Response(asset.body, { headers: { "content-type": asset.contentType } });',
    '  },',
    '};',
  ].join('\n')
}

/** Workers-for-Platforms script upload is a multipart form: metadata + the module. */
const multipartBody = (metadata: unknown, script: string): string => {
  const delimiter = `--${MULTIPART_BOUNDARY}`
  return [
    delimiter,
    'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="metadata"',
    'Content-Type: application/json',
    '',
    JSON.stringify(metadata),
    delimiter,
    `Content-Disposition: form-data; name="${WORKER_ENTRY}"; filename="${WORKER_ENTRY}"`,
    'Content-Type: application/javascript+module',
    '',
    script,
    `${delimiter}--`,
    '',
  ].join(CRLF)
}

/**
 * Cloudflare's OWN error text, sliced. `details` is persisted into `sites.deploy`, so it
 * carries the provider message and nothing of ours — no request, no token material.
 */
const providerDetail = (body: string): string | undefined => {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(body) as { errors?: Array<{ message?: unknown }> }
    const message = parsed.errors?.[0]?.message
    return typeof message === 'string' ? message.slice(0, DETAIL_MAX) : undefined
  } catch {
    return undefined
  }
}

/** A 2xx is only a success if the envelope says so AND names the uploaded script. */
const isUploadAccepted = (body: string): boolean => {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(body) as { success?: unknown; result?: unknown }
    return (
      parsed.success === true &&
      typeof parsed.result === 'object' &&
      parsed.result !== null &&
      typeof (parsed.result as { id?: unknown }).id === 'string'
    )
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

const httpErrorCode = (status: number): ErrorCode =>
  status === 429 ? 'RATE_LIMITED' : 'PROVIDER_ERROR'

/**
 * Deploys the bundle as a Workers-for-Platforms dispatch-namespace script. Written in
 * full against recorded fixtures with ZERO live calls in tests, exactly how the X and
 * GBP adapters shipped. Enabling it in production is credentials, not code — and when
 * those credentials are absent it returns a typed error, NEVER a fake success.
 */
export const createCloudflareDeployer = (deps: CloudflareDeployerDeps): Deployer => {
  return async (bundle: SiteBundle, ctx: DeployContext): Promise<Result<SiteDeployState>> => {
    const now = ctx.now ?? deps.now ?? (() => new Date())
    const config = deps.config
    if (!config) {
      // No credentials means no site. Returning ok here would resurrect the v1 fake-deploy.
      return err(appError('PROVIDER_ERROR', NOT_CONFIGURED, ctx.traceId, { siteId: ctx.siteId }))
    }

    const scriptName = `${SCRIPT_NAME_PREFIX}${ctx.slug}`
    const endpoint = `${CF_API_ROOT}/accounts/${config.accountId}/workers/dispatch/namespaces/${config.dispatchNamespace}/scripts/${scriptName}`
    const body = multipartBody(
      { main_module: WORKER_ENTRY, compatibility_date: COMPATIBILITY_DATE, bindings: [] },
      workerSource(bundle.files),
    )

    let res: TransportResponse
    try {
      res = await deps.transport({
        method: 'PUT',
        url: endpoint,
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${config.apiToken}`,
          'content-type': `multipart/form-data; boundary=${MULTIPART_BOUNDARY}`,
        },
        body,
      })
    } catch (error) {
      // No response arrived. Persist ONLY the error name: a misbehaving transport can echo
      // the Authorization header into `err.message`, and `details` lands in sites.deploy.
      return err(
        appError(
          'PROVIDER_ERROR',
          'Cloudflare upload failed before a response was received.',
          ctx.traceId,
          { name: error instanceof Error ? error.name : 'unknown' },
        ),
      )
    }

    if (res.status < 200 || res.status >= 300) {
      return err(
        appError(
          httpErrorCode(res.status),
          `Cloudflare rejected the upload with HTTP ${res.status}.`,
          ctx.traceId,
          { status: res.status, detail: providerDetail(res.body) },
        ),
      )
    }

    if (!isUploadAccepted(res.body)) {
      return err(
        appError(
          'PROVIDER_ERROR',
          'Cloudflare returned a success status without a usable result.',
          ctx.traceId,
          { status: res.status, bodySample: res.body.slice(0, DETAIL_MAX) },
        ),
      )
    }

    const deployedAt = now().toISOString()
    const url = `https://${ctx.slug}.${ctx.baseDomain ?? config.baseDomain}`

    return ok({
      deployer: DEPLOYER_NAME,
      status: 'live',
      preview: false,
      url,
      bundleId: bundle.bundleId,
      scriptName,
      deployedAt,
      error: null,
      history: appendHistory(ctx.previous, {
        bundleId: bundle.bundleId,
        deployedAt,
        url,
        preview: false,
      }),
    })
  }
}
```
